A reader adds a book with a title and author, views the books in the order added, and removes books. It exists so the reader can track what to read next without losing order or accepting incomplete entries.\n\n## What It Does\n\nA reader adds a book with a title and an author. A reader adds a book with a title and author, views the books in the order added, and removes books. It exists so the reader can track what to read next without losing order or accepting incomplete entries.\n\n## What It Does\n\nA reader adds a book with a title and an author.
